======================================================================
CYBER SECURITY KNOWLEDGE BASE

Entry 1
Category: Cyber Security Fundamentals
Concept: Cyber Security
Definition: The practice of protecting systems, networks and data from cyber threats.
Key Points:
- Essential concept in cyber security.
- Frequently covered in certifications and technical interviews.
- Important for protecting systems, applications and data.
Example:
Cyber Security is widely used in modern cyber security.
Related Concepts:
Cryptography, Network Security, Authentication, Risk Management, Incident Response

Entry 36
Category: Common Attacks
Concept: SQL Injection
Definition: Injects malicious SQL into database queries.
Key Points:
- Essential concept in cyber security.
- Frequently covered in certifications and technical interviews.
- Important for protecting systems, applications and data.
Example:
SELECT * FROM users WHERE id='1' OR '1'='1';
Related Concepts:
Cryptography, Network Security, Authentication, Risk Management, Incident Response

Entry 37
Category: Common Attacks
Concept: Cross-Site Scripting
Definition: Injects malicious scripts into web pages.
Key Points:
- Essential concept in cyber security.
- Frequently covered in certifications and technical interviews.
- Important for protecting systems, applications and data.
Example:
<script>alert('XSS')</script>
Related Concepts:
Cryptography, Network Security, Authentication, Risk Management, Incident Response
